[-07-17 23:49] (think): The user wants to know what this skill is about.
[-07-17 23:49] TEXT: 这个 **apple-design** skill 是一份关于 Apple 风格界面设计和流畅动效的实现指南，来源于 WWDC 设计演讲（主要是 2018 年的 *Designing Fluid Interfaces*），并翻译为 Web 平台的 CSS/JS 实现方案。 ## 核心理念 **让界面感觉像物理世界的延伸**——运动是连续的、可中断的、有惯性的，而不是脚本化的固定动画。 ## 它会帮你实现什么 主要覆盖 17 个方面： 1. **即时响应** — pointer-down 时立即反馈，不等 click 2. **直接操纵** — 1:1 跟踪手指/鼠标，尊重抓取偏移量 3. **可中断性**（最重要） — 任何动画都能中途抓起并反转，从当前屏幕值而非目标值开始 4. **弹簧动画（Springs）** — 用阻尼比+响应时间替代固定时长，支持 Apple 推荐的具体数值（如 `damping 1.0, response 0.4`） 5. **速度继承** — 手势结束时动画继承手指速度，无视觉断裂 6. **惯性投射** — 根据释放速度预测落点，而非简单吸附最近边界 7. **空间一致性** — 进出路径对称，菜单从触发元素展开 8. **橡胶回弹（Rubber-banding）** — 边界处渐进阻力而非硬停 9. **多模态反馈** — 视觉+声音+触觉在同一帧触发 10. **减弱动效 & 无障碍** — `prefers-reduced-motion`、`prefers-reduced-transparency` 等 11. **排版** — 字间距随字号变化、行高反向缩放、使用系统字体 12. **材质与深度** — 半透明层（`backdrop-filter: blur()`）传达层级 13. **手势设计细节** — 迟滞阈值、并行手势识别、双击延迟优化 14. **帧级流畅度** — 只动画 `transform` 和 `opacity`，使用 `will-change` 15. **Apple 八大设计原则** — Purpose / Agency / Responsibility / Familiarity / Flexibility / Simplicity / Craft / Delight ## 实际用途 当你需要实现以下交互时，这个 skill 会提供具体的 CSS/JS 代码片段和参数值： - 拖拽抽屉/底部面板（sheet） - 可中断的弹簧过渡动画 - 滚动手势和惯性滚动 - 半透明导航栏/工具栏 - 减弱动效的无障碍降级 - 符合 Apple 审美的排版系统 简单说：**它是一份从 Apple 设计哲学到 Web 代码的完整映射手册**，不是生成 UI 的工具，而是指导你在实现交互时做出正确的动效和设计决策。

[-07-18 10:04] (think): The user wants to know what these 5 skills do. Let me summarize each one clearly.
[-07-18 10:04] TEXT: 这 5 个 skill 构成了一套完整的**动效设计工程工具链**，全部基于 Emil Kowalski（Sonner 作者，animations.dev）的设计工程哲学。它们各司其职： --- ## 1. `emil-design-eng` — 动效设计工程知识库 **是什么**：一份详尽的设计工程手册，包含动效决策框架、组件构建原则、CSS 技巧和性能规则。 **帮你做什么**： - 判断一个元素**该不该加动画**（频率表：100+/天的不加，偶尔的可加） - 选择正确的 **easing 曲线**（`ease-out` 进入，不用 `ease-in`） - 设置正确的 **duration**（UI 控件 <300ms） - 用 **spring** 替代固定时长动画 - 组件级技巧：按钮 `:active` 缩放、popover 从触发点展开、tooltip 跳过后续延迟、`clip-path` 做标签页颜色过渡和长按删除 - **性能**：只动画 `transform` 和 `opacity`，Framer Motion 硬件加速注意事项 - **无障碍**：`prefers-reduced-motion`、触摸设备 hover 门控 --- ## 2. `review-animations` — 动效代码审查 **是什么**：专门审查动效代码的 review skill，采用严格标准，**默认不通过，通过需要挣得**。 **帮你做什么**： - 拿 10 条**不可妥协的标准**逐条审查（easing、duration、origin、interruptibility、GPU、a11y 等） - 输出 **Before/After/Why 表格**，每个问题一行 - 给出分级结论（Feel-breaking / 简化机会 / 性能 / 中断性 / 物理正确性 / 无障碍） - 最终判定 **Block** 或 **Approve** 典型发现：`transition: all`、`scale(0)` 入场、`ease-in` 用于 UI、键盘操作加了动画、缺少 `prefers-reduced-motion` --- ## 3. `improve-animations` — 动效审计与改进规划 **是什么**：对整个代码库做动效审计，输出优先级排序的改进计划。**只读，不改代码**。 **帮你做什么**： - **Phase 1 Recon**：摸清技术栈、动效库、已有的 easing token、产品性格、频率图 - **Phase 2 Audit**：并行审查 8 个维度（目的、缓动、物理性、中断性、性能、无障碍、一致性、遗漏机会） - **Phase 3 Vet**：逐条核实，按杠杆率排序，输出 Finding 表 + 被拒候选 - **Phase 4 Plans**：为选中的 finding 写自包含的实施计划（精确到文件路径、代码片段、目标值），存入 `plans/` 目录 适合场景：接手一个项目想系统性提升动效质量，或让更便宜的模型按计划执行修复。 --- ## 4. `find-animation-opportunities` — 发现该加动画的地方 **是什么**：搜索代码库，找出**没加动画但应该加**的地方。**只读，只提议**。 **帮你做什么**： - 扫描 6 类机会：反馈缺失（按钮无 `:active`）、瞬移状态（内容突然出现/消失）、缺少空间故事（popover 无触发点关联）、分组入场（列表一次性弹出无 stagger）、手势接缝（拖拽无物理感）、delight 预算（首次运行/空状态/成功状态） - 每个候选经过 4 道**门控**：频率 → 目的 → 速度 → 功能性 - 最终输出：5-7 个高确信度建议 + 被拒候选列表 + 结论 - 核心原则：**克制**。大部分地方不该加动画 --- ## 5. `animation-vocabulary` — 动效术语反向词典 **是什么**：把模糊的动效描述翻译成精确术语。纯知识查询，不写代码。 **帮你做什么**： - "那个弹出框从按钮长出来的东西" → **Origin-aware animation** - "iOS 拉过头会弹回来" → **Rubber-banding** - "一个图变成另一个图" → **Morph**（区分 Crossfade / Shared element transition） - 涵盖完整词汇表：Entrances & Exits、Sequencing、Transforms、Transitions、Scroll、Feedback、Easing、Springs、Looping、Polish、Performance、Principles --- ## 它们怎么配合 ``` animation-vocabulary → 你说不清效果叫什么时，先查术语 ↓ find-animation-opportunities → 扫描代码库，找出该加动画的地方 ↓ emil-design-eng → 查具体的实现参数和原则 ↓ improve-animations → 输出可执行的改进计划 ↓ review-animations → 审查实现质量，给 Block/Approve ```

[-07-18 10:11] TEXT: I now have a comprehensive picture. Here is the full audit report. --- ## Animation Audit Report: prism-agent ### 1. Animation Token System **Easing Curves (defined in `animation.css`):** | Token | Value | Purpose | |-------|-------|---------| | `--ease-out` | `cubic-bezier(0.23, 1, 0.32, 1)` | UI interactions, fast start, responsive feel | | `--ease-in-out` | `cubic-bezier(0.77, 0, 0.175, 1)` | Moving/deforming elements | | `--ease-drawer` | `cubic-bezier(0.32, 0.72, 0, 1)` | iOS-style drawer (Ionic) | | `--spring-bounce` | `cubic-bezier(0.34, 1.56, 0.64, 1)` | Momentum interactions with overshoot | | `--spring-default` | `cubic-bezier(0.23, 1, 0.32, 1)` | Apple critical damping (damping 1.0, response 0.3) | | `--spring-momentum` | `cubic-bezier(0.34, 1.56, 0.64, 1)` | Apple slight bounce (damping 0.8, response 0.35) | | `--spring-snappy` | `cubic-bezier(0.16, 1, 0.3, 1)` | Apple fast response (damping 1.0, response 0.15) | **Duration Tokens:** **NONE.** There are no shared `--duration-*` CSS variables anywhere. Every animation hardcodes its duration inline (e.g., `0.2s`, `0.15s`, `200ms`, `250ms`, `280ms`, `300ms`, `0.8s`, `1.5s`, `3s`). This is a significant gap in the token system. **Keyframe Tokens (defined in `theme.css`):** | Token | Duration | Easing | Purpose | |-------|----------|--------|---------| | `--animate-checkbox-bounce` | 300ms | `cubic-bezier(0.4, 0, 0.2, 1)` | Checkbox press feedback | | `--animate-checkbox-icon-in` | 160ms | `ease-out` | Check icon fade-in | **`tw-animate-css` (v1.3.8):** Imported in `tailwind.css` and the UI library's storybook. Provides Tailwind utility classes for animations like `animate-in`, `animate-out`, `fade-in-0`, `fade-out-0`, `zoom-in-95`, `zoom-out-95`, `slide-in-from-*`, `accordion-down`, `accordion-up`. These are used extensively in Dialog, Popover, DropdownMenu, Select, Tooltip, Drawer, and ContextMenu components. The library provides CSS-only keyframes with configurable duration/easing via Tailwind `duration-*` and `ease-*` utilities. **Assessment:** The project has a two-tier system: - **Custom easing tokens** (`--ease-out`, `--spring-*`) in `animation.css` -- well-named, Apple-inspired - **No duration tokens** -- every component hardcodes durations ad hoc - **No motion token file** -- the token system (`packages/ui/src/styles/tokens/`) covers colors, spacing, radius, and typography, but has **zero motion/animation tokens** --- ### 2. Global Animation Issues **Issue A: Duplicated `prefers-reduced-motion` rules (3 locations)** 1. `src/renderer/assets/styles/animation.css` -- per-animation blocks (11 separate `@media` blocks) 2. `src/renderer/assets/styles/index.css` (line 238-246) -- global override: `animation-duration: 0.01ms !important; transition-duration: 0.01ms !important;` 3. `src/renderer/assets/styles/tailwind.css` (line 190-198) -- **duplicate** of the above: `animation-duration: 0.01ms !important; transition-duration: 0.01ms !important;` The per-animation blocks in `animation.css` are redundant because the global rule in `index.css` already kills all animations/transitions. The `tailwind.css` version is an exact copy. [-07-18 10:12] TEXT: I now have a comprehensive picture of the entire animation landscape. Let me compile the final audit report. --- # Animation Audit Report — Apple Design Principles ## Executive Summary The app has a well-defined Apple Design spring preset library (`src/renderer/lib/appleDesign.ts`) with `SPRING_DEFAULT`, `SPRING_SNAPPY`, `SPRING_GENTLE`, etc. However, **only 1 of the ~40+ dialog/modal/popup/selector components actually uses it** (CollapsibleSearchBar). The core UI primitives (Dialog, Popover, Select, HoverCard, DropdownMenu) all use Tailwind CSS keyframe animations (`zoom-in-95`, `fade-in-0`) with a hardcoded `duration-200`, which produces a mechanical, non-physical feel that violates Apple's spring-based motion language. --- ## Findings Table | # | File:Line | Issue (Before) | Fix (After) | Why (Apple Design) | |---|-----------|---------------|-------------|-------------------| | 1 | `packages/ui/src/components/primitives/dialog.tsx:95` | Dialog uses `duration-200` with CSS keyframes `zoom-in-95 fade-in-0` — linear timing, no spring physics | Replace with `framer-motion` `<AnimatePresence>` + `motion.div` using `SPRING_GENTLE` preset, or use `data-[state=open]:animate-in` with a custom spring `@keyframes` (damping-based) | Apple modals use spring physics (damping ~1.0, response ~0.35) for organic deceleration. A flat 200ms ease feels mechanical. This is the **single highest-impact change** since every modal in the app inherits from this. | | 2 | `packages/ui/src/components/primitives/dialog.tsx:30` | Dialog overlay uses `duration-200 fade-in-0 fade-out-0` | Use `SPRING_GENTLE` timing for overlay opacity, or at minimum increase duration to ~300ms for a slower, more deliberate fade | Apple overlays (sheet dimming) animate slowly and deliberately, not in a quick 200ms snap. | | 3 | `packages/ui/src/components/primitives/popover.tsx:37` | Popover uses `zoom-in-95 fade-in-0` + directional slide with no explicit spring | Replace with `SPRING_DEFAULT` preset animation. The `origin-(--radix-popover-content-transform-origin)` is correct and Apple-aligned, but the timing is wrong | Apple popovers grow from their trigger point with spring damping. The origin-awareness is good; the linear 200ms timing is not. | | 4 | `packages/ui/src/components/primitives/select.tsx:203` | SelectContent uses same `zoom-in-95 fade-in-0` pattern | Same fix as Popover — use spring-based animation with `SPRING_DEFAULT` | Select dropdowns should feel like they "pop" from the trigger, not mechanically fade in. | | 5 | `packages/ui/src/components/primitives/hover-card.tsx:35` | HoverCard uses same `zoom-in-95 fade-in-0` pattern | Use `SPRING_GENTLE` (response 0.4) for the larger hover card surface | Larger surfaces should use gentler, slower springs per Apple HIG. | | 6 | `packages/ui/src/components/primitives/dropdown-menu.tsx:38` | DropdownMenu uses same `zoom-in-95 fade-in-0` pattern | Use `SPRING_SNAPPY` (response 0.15) for tight, responsive menus | Dropdown menus are small and action-oriented; a snappy spring communicates precision. | | 7 | `src/renderer/components/ContentSearch.tsx:467` | `transition-all ease-in-out` on `SearchBarContainer` — broad property, wrong easing | `transition-[opacity,transform] duration-200` with `ease-out` or spring via `motion` | `transition-all` animates every property (including layout-triggering ones), causing jank. Apple uses `ease-out` for appear/disappear, not `ease-in-out`. | | 8 | `src/renderer/components/ContentSearch.tsx:355`  …(+6713)
